def build_A(p):
    n=p.size
    A=np.zeros((2*n-1,n), dtype=np.float64)
    # for j in range(n): col j contains p[k-j] for k=j..j+n-1
    # We can use strides or loop (n small)
    for j in range(n):
        A[j:j+n, j] = p
    return A
```

Detailed functions:
```
def _fast_score(a):
    a=np.asarray...
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return inf
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=1e-15: return inf
    a=a/s  # normalize for score invariance
    c=np.convolve(a,a)
    return 2*n*np.max(c)
```
Weights: If we normalize to sum1 repeatedly, score invariant but numerical stable. Evaluator also clips before sum; if a values ≤1, okay. Return float.

`_exact_score(a)` just call evaluate_sequence([float(v) for v in a])? Use list conversion. For p length up to 256, okay. We'll call not too often.

`_accept(p,best)`: p normalized, fast score; if finite and <best[0]-1e-12: exact = evaluate; if exact < best[0]: update. But if fast< best but exact not (due evaluator clipping? no), no update. If fast >= best but due exact? Not needed.

LP solver helper:
```
def _lp_minmax(p, time_limit_per_lp):
    n=p.size
    A=np.zeros((2*n-1,n))
    # fill columns
    for j in range(n): A[j:j+n,j]=p
    A2=np.hstack((A, -np.ones((A.shape[0],1))))
    c=np.zeros(n+1); c[-1]=1
    Aeq=np.array([[np.ones(n),0.0]])
    beq=np.array([1.0])
    res=linprog(c, A_ub=A2, b_ub=np.zeros(A.shape[0]), A_eq=Aeq, b_eq=beq, bounds=[(0,None)]*n+[(None,None)?], method='highs', options={'presolve':True})
    if res.status !=0: return None
    x=res.x
    g=x[:n]; t=x[-1]
    g=np.clip(g,0,None); sg=g.sum(); if sg<=0: return None
    g/=sg
    return g
```
For t bound, can allow negative? t should be ≥0, but if constraints b_ub=0 and A_p nonnegative, t must ≥0 if g nonzero. Bound (0,None) okay. Could allow t None for efficiency; but t negative infeasible? A_p g -t ≤0 with A_p g≥0 => t≥max A_p g≥0, so no need lower bound? If t can be negative, constraints force it. But objective min t, it won't be negative. Bound None avoids? either.

Local stagnation handling:
- Keep `stall` count.
- Each iteration choose direction based on `stall`:
   - if stall==0: LP minmax.
   - if stall==1: LP cap with M=current max*0.95 or target*1.02.
   - if stall==2: gradient direction.
   - if stall==3: random multiplicative perturbation.
   - if stall>5: try smaller LP step via cap M=current max (max-sum), etc.
- If no improvement after max_stall (e.g., 12), break or new start.

Let's define `_smooth_logsumexp(c, mu)`:
```
shift=np.max(c)
z=(c-shift)/mu
z=np.clip(z, -50, 50)
s=np.sum(np.exp(z))
return mu*(np.log(s)+shift), np.exp(z)/s
```
If μ extremely small, exponent may overflow; clip. Since c max shift, z≤0, no overflow; only underflow. Good.

Detailed `fast_score`:
```
def _fast_score(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a=np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0, posinf=0, neginf=0)
    a=np.clip(a,0,None)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return inf
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=1e-15: return inf
    a=a/s
    c=np.convolve(a,a)
    m=float(np.max(c))
    if not math.isfinite(m): return inf
    return float(2.0*n*m)
```
Because sum=1, no divide. For evaluator sum can be any; score invariant exactly if sum>0. But if a clipped by evaluator to 1000 after sum? We normalize to sum1 so no clip issue. Good.

`_try_update`:
```
def _try_update(best, arr):
    try:
       a=np.asarray(arr,...)
       a=np.clip(a,0,1000.0)
       if a.size==0: return
       s=a.sum()
       if s<=1e-15: return
       a=a/s
       fs=_fast_score(a)
       if math.isfinite(fs) and fs < best[0] - 1e-12:
           es=evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in a])
           if math.isfinite(es) and es < best[0]:
              best[0]=float(es); best[1]=a.copy()
    except: pass
```
But if fs equal but exact due rounding? okay. We can also update if a is parent with exact lower even if fast not (shouldn't).

def _make_starts(n, rng, parent):
   starts=[]
   def add(a): clean normalize; if size n and finite append
   add(np.ones(n)/n)
   x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   for shift in (0.5,1.0,1.5,2.0):
      for alpha in (0.35,0.4,0.45,0.5,0.55,0.6):
         g=(np.arange(n)+shift)**(-alpha)
         add(g)
   for a in (0.5,1.0):
      add(np.diff(np.sqrt(np.arange(n+1)+a)))  # length n
   # symmetric sums
   x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   for c in (0.5,1.0,2.0,-0.5,-1.0): add(np.exp(c*(x-0.5)))
   for k in (1,2,3,5): add(1+0.7*np.sin(2πk(x-0.5)))
   # parent interpolation
   if parent is not None:
       gp=np.interp(np.linspace(0,1,n), np.linspace(0,1,parent.size), parent)
       add(gp)
       # shifted parent? maybe
   # random
   for alpha in (0.1,0.25,0.5,2): add(rng.dirichlet(np.full(n,alpha)))
   # score sort return top
   scored=[]
   for a in starts: sc=_fast_score(a); if finite scored.append((sc,a))
   sorted; unique? Could have duplicates; not important. Return top 8.
```

          stall+=1

      stall+=1

Now, implement line search carefully.
```
def _line_search_dir(p, q, c_p=None, c_q=None, c_m=None):
    n=p.size
    if c_p is None: c_p=np.convolve(p,p)
    if c_q is None: c_q=np.convolve(q,q)
    if c_m is None: c_m=np.convolve(p,q)
    L=2*n-1
    # candidate tau grid
    taus = np.array([0.0, 0.005,0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5,0.75,1.0])
    # Also include around current? no.
    T=taus.size
    C=np.empty((T,L), dtype=np.float64)
    one=1.0
    for i,tau in enumerate(taus):
       C[i]=((1-tau)**2)*c_p + (2*tau*(1-tau))*c_m + (tau**2)*c_q
    M=C.max(axis=1)
    idx=int(np.argmin(M))
    best_tau=float(taus[idx]); best_sc=float(M[idx])
    # local refine
    if 0 < idx < len(taus)-1:
       lo=taus[idx-1]; hi=taus[idx+1]
    elif idx==0:
       lo=0.0; hi=taus[1]
    else:
       lo=taus[-2]; hi=1.0
    if hi>lo:
       taus2=np.linspace(lo,hi,33)
       C2=...
       M2=...
       idx2=argmin
       best_tau=float(taus2[idx2]); best_sc=float(M2[idx2])
       # second refine between neighbors
       if 0<idx2<32:
          lo2=taus2[idx2-1]; hi2=taus2[idx2+1]
          taus3=np.linspace(lo2,hi2,21)
          ...
    candidate = (1-best_tau)*p + best_tau*q
    candidate = normalize(candidate)
    # In formula sum already 1 if p,q sum1; but numerical.
    sc = fast_score(candidate)
    # If grid score computed M*? Score =2n*M; line search returns M. Use fast for consistency.
    return candidate, sc
```
Potential performance: Allocating C for each line search 11*191 small. For 1000 calls, 2M floats ~16MB total sequential, okay. But repeated allocation overhead maybe. We can avoid full C for first grid by loop; but fine.

Need ensure if p and q not normalized? We'll normalize before.

Potential problem: `linprog` with dense A and equality may be slow due presolve. We can pre-solve? Let's implement minimal.
```
def _lp_minmax(p):
    n=p.size
    A=_build_A(p)
    # Instead of hstack each time, use A_ub = A; add t variable separately? SciPy requires same columns. Could use A_ub = np.column_stack((A, -np.ones((rows,1))))
    c = np.concatenate((np.zeros(n), [1.0]))
    Aeq = np.concatenate((np.ones(n), [0.0]))
    bounds = [(0.0, None)]*n + [(0.0, None)]
    res = linprog(c, A_ub=A2, b_ub=np.zeros(A.shape[0]), A_eq=Aeq.reshape(1,-1), b_eq=np.array([1.0]), bounds=bounds, method='highs')
```
Could reduce bounds overhead for large n by using `bounds = [(0.0, None)] * (n+1)`; t lower0. fine.

`_lp_cap(p,M)`:
```
A=_build_A(p); c=-ones(n); res=linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=np.full(rows,M), bounds=[(0,None)]*n, method='highs', options=...)
g=res.x; sg=sum(g); if sg<=0 return None; return g/sg
```
If M too small but nonzero, g finite? Constraints A g≤M; g can be unbounded? Rows with all zeros? Are there zero rows? Row0 has p0; if p0=0, row0 could be all 0? For p0=0, row0: col0 p0=0, others invalid -> all zero. Then g0 unbounded; linprog may return unbounded? def _lbfgs_polish(p0, deadline, rng, best):
   if minimize is None: return
   n=p0.size
   x=np.log(p0+1e-300)
   for mu_factor in (0.05,0.02,0.008,0.003,0.001,0.0004):
      if time.monotonic() >= deadline-20: break
      def fun(x, mu=mu_factor*initialM?):
         y=x-np.max(x); p=np.exp(y); p/=p.sum()
         c=np.convolve(p,p)
         shift=np.max(c); z=(c-shift)/mu
         z=np.clip(z, -50, 50)
         e=np.exp(z); s=e.sum()

def _lp_refine(p0, deadline, rng, best, tag='lp'):
    p = normalize(p0)
    local = _fast_score(p)
    _try_update(best,p)
    target_r = TARGET/(2*p.size)
    stall=0
    iter=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 2:
        iter +=1
        c_p=np.convolve(p,p); M=float(np.max(c_p))
        if not finite: break
        improved=False
        # choose actions
        g=None
        if stall % 3 == 0:
            tol = min(deadline, time.monotonic()+?) # no
            g=_lp_minmax(p)
        elif stall % 3 == 1:
            Mcap = max(target_r*1.0005, 0.985*M)
            if Mcap > M: Mcap=M #? if target above current? Actually target_r < M if not met. If M below target, use M*(1-0.001)
            if Mcap <=0: Mcap=1e-12
            g=_lp_cap(p, Mcap)
        else:
            Mcap = min(M, max(target_r*0.999, 0.96*M)) maybe
            g=_lp_cap(p, Mcap)
        if g is None:
            # try smaller cap?
        if g is not None:
            q=normalize(g)
            if np.linalg.norm(q-p) > 1e-8:
                cand, sc = _line_search(p,q,c_p=c_p) # q c not cached
                if cand is not None and sc < local - 1e-12:
                    p=cand; local=sc; stall=0; _try_update(best,p); improved=True
        if not improved:
            stall += 1
            if stall == 6:
                # perturb
                pp = p * np.exp(0.05*rng.standard_normal(n))
                pp=normalize(pp); ps=_fast_score(pp)
                if ps < local*1.01: p=pp; local=ps
            if stall >= 25:
                break
        if iter % 50 == 0:
            # maybe try exact line search with g q from previous? no
```

def _mirror_refine(p0, deadline, rng, best, max_mu_factor=0.05, min_mu_factor=1e-6):
    p=normalize(p0); local=fast
    x=np.log(p+1e-300)
    c=conv; mu=0.05*max(c) or 1e-6
    stall=0
    for it in range(5000):
       if time >= deadline-1: break
       c=conv(p,p); M=max(c)
       if M <=0: break
       mu=max(1e-12, min(mu, 0.1*M))? We want decreasing. Initially 0.05M. Each accepted maybe mu*=0.95.
       J,w=logsumexp(c, mu)
       gp=2*np.correlate(w,p,'valid')
       # Normalize direction: d = gp / max(gp) maybe to scale eta independent.
       gmax=np.max(gp)
       if not finite or gmax<=0: break
       d=gp/gmax
       # Try eta grid
       bestcand=p; bestsc=local
       for eta in (1e-4,1e-3,3e-3,1e-2,3e-2,0.1,0.3,1.0):
           y=x-eta*d
           y-=y.max(); q=np.exp(y); qs=q.sum(); if qs<=0: continue; q=q/qs
           sc=fast(q)
           if sc<bestsc: bestsc=sc; bestcand=q
       if bestcand is not p:
           p=bestcand; local=bestsc; x=np.log(p+1e-300); stall=0; mu*=0.9
           _try_update(best,p)
           if local <= TARGET/(2*n) maybe etc:
       else:
           stall+=1
           if stall%5==0:
              x += 0.02*rng.standard_normal(n)
           if stall>30: break
```

```
def _lbfgs_polish(p0, deadline, rng, best):
    if minimize is None: return
    p=normalize(p0); local=fast
    M0=max(conv(p,p)); if M0<=0 return
    x=np.log(p+1e-300)
    for f in (0.05,0.02,0.008,0.003,0.001,0.0004,0.0001):
       if time >= deadline-10: break
       mu=f*M0
       def fun(xval):
          y=xval-np.max(xval); pv=np.exp(y); s=pv.sum(); if s<=0: pv=np.ones(n)/n else pv/=s
          c=np.convolve(pv,pv); shift=c.max(); z=(c-shift)/mu; e=np.exp(np.clip(z,-50,0?)) # z<=0
          ssum=e.sum(); if ssum<=0: val=np.max(c); w=np.zeros(L); w.argmax=1?; else val=mu*(np.log(ssum)+shift); w=e/ssum
          # optional: if mu tiny and ssum underflow, w active
          gp=2*np.correlate(w,pv,'valid'); G=np.dot(pv,gp); gx=pv*(gp-G)
          return val, gx
       try res=minimize(fun, x, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter':40,'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-8,'maxfun':150})
       except: break
       if res.x is not None:
           q=softmax(res.x); cq? line search p->q
           cand, sc=_line_search(p,q)
           if cand is not None and sc < local - 1e-12:
              p=cand; local=sc; _try_update; x=np.log(p+eps)
           # else keep p
       # decay? if improved, continue
       if local <= target*(1+1e-7)? Could still continue.
```

Implement inside `_local_refine`:
```
def _local_refine(p0, dl, rng, best):
  p=normalize(p0); local=fast; _try_update
  total=dl-time.monotonic()
  if total<5: return p
  t1=min(dl, time.monotonic()+0.78*total)
  p = _lp_refine(p, t1, rng,best, n)
  total=dl-time.monotonic()
  if total>30 and n<=128:
     t2=min(dl, time.monotonic()+0.25*total)
     p=_lbfgs_polish(p,t2,rng,best)
  total=dl-time.monotonic()
  if total>15 and n<=160:
     t3=min(dl, time.monotonic()+0.5*total)
     p=_mirror_refine(p,t3,rng,best)
  total=dl-time.monotonic()
  if total>30:
     p=_lp_refine(p, min(dl, time.monotonic()+total), rng,best,n)
  return p
```
Need avoid phases overlapping. Use `min(dl, now + frac*remaining_at_phase)` dynamic.

But if LP stalls after 50s (stall break), remaining total large; L-BFGS gets large fraction. Good.

In `_lp_refine`, if p already at target maybe can still break? It could continue to improve. But if local score <= TARGET and improvements tiny, we can limit iterations. We can break if stall > 30 or local <= TARGET and no improvement for 10.

LP loop maximum iterations? Could run long if tiny improvements. Set `max_iter=5000` but break on time. If no improvement stall>30 break. If improvement each time but tiny, could continue; okay until time.

We need c_p in LP loop. We can compute before direction. If line search improves, c_p update next loop. Good.

Now, possible speed improvement: In `_lp_refine`, line search computes c_q/c_m. For LP direction, q often is LP solution. We can compute c_q (since needed for exact score) and c_m via np.convolve. That's two conv plus p conv. Each n=96 conv cheap. LP solve likely more expensive.

Now, let's examine exact derivation of derivative for L-BFGS to ensure no missing factor. J = μ logsumexp(c/μ). Let c_j = Σ_i p_i p_{j-i}. ∂c_j/∂p_k = ? We derived 2 p_{j-k} if 0≤j-k<n. Let's validate with c2 derivative p1: c2=2p0p2+p1^2. ∂=2p1. Formula j=2,k=1: 2p_{1}=2p1. Good. c3 with p=(a,b,c,d): c3=2ab? Wait pairs sum3: (0,3),(1,2),(2,1),(3,0)=2a d +2 b c. ∂ wrt p1 (b): 2c. Formula j=3,k=1: 2p_{2}=2c. Good. So gp = 2 * correlate(w,p,valid) where correlate index i sum w_i? `np.correlate(w,p,valid)[k] = Σ_{m=0}^{n-1} w[k+m] p[m]`. This matches Σ_j w_j p_{j-k}. Good.

For L-BFGS gradient wrt x: `gx = p * (gp - dot(p,gp))`. Check with simple J=p0 (linear). gp=[1,0..]. D=p1? dot(p,gp)=p0. gx=p*(gp-p0). For l=0: p0*(1-p0); other: -p_l p0. Sum zero. Good. If x changes, p0 roughly p0(1-ηp0)? okay.

For mirror update using gp directly: The natural exponential family dual variable is `theta = -η gp`, update p=exp(theta)/sum with base θ? If we set x <- x - η gp, that's exactly x_new = x - η gp (not softmax gradient). This is different but common multiplicative weights for minimizing linear functions; for smooth okay. We line search exact, so okay.

Could mirror update be too strong if gp large? We normalized d=gp/gmax. With max active gp ~? If active lag has p_{j-k} maybe p0. For target p shape, p0~0.08, gp max maybe0.08; gmax 0.08; d entries 0-1. eta 0.3 changes central. Good.

Need include a floor to prevent x from going too negative/positive causing exp underflow. In softmax stable, if y max high, some exp(very negative) underflow to0, fine. If y max = -1e300? x from log p can be -300. y subtract max, okay. If all y underflow? exp(0)=1 for max. Good.

Now, consider using random perturb in LP stall: We should preserve local best p if perturb bad. In code, if perturb score < local*1.05 maybe accept just to move; but not update global best. This can escape local. But if it worsens local significantly, could reduce potential. Use small noise and only accept if score not much worse (< local*1.02). Then set local=ps. If later can't recover, we lost original p. Better keep a `base_p` = best local seen; after several bad perturb, revert to base. Implement:

base_p=p.copy(); base_local=local
...

```
Simpler: keep `p_best_local` unchanged; if perturb improves or slightly worse than current but not base, accept temporary. If stall remains, break. But if base_local low, we don't want to set local worse too much. We can set if `ps < min(local*1.02, base_local*1.05)`. Good.
After perturb, set `p_base`? If later improve, update base. Let's implement:

best_p_local=p.copy(); best_local=local
...
improved -> best_p_local=p; best_local=local; stall=0

import numpy as np

Clean and normalize:
```
def _clean_arr(x):
    try:
       a=np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
       return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size==0: a=np.array([1.0])
    a=np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a=np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return a

def _normalize1(a):
    a=_clean_arr(a)
    s=float(np.sum(a))
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-15:
       return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return a / s
```
If size 0? _clean ensures.

Fast score:
```
def _fast_score(a):
    a=np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a=np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,None)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return math.inf
    s=float(np.sum(a))
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s<=1e-15: return math.inf
    a=a/s
    c=np.convolve(a,a)
    if c.size==0: return math.inf
    m=float(np.max(c))
    if not math.isfinite(m): return math.inf
    return float(2.0*n*m)
```

best update:
```
def _try_update(best, cand):
    try:
      a=np.asarray(cand,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
      a=np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
      a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
      if a.size==0: return
      s=float(np.sum(a))
      if not math.isfinite(s) or s<=1e-15: return
      a=a/s
      fs=_fast_score(a)
      if math.isfinite(fs) and fs < best[0] - 1e-12:
         es=evaluate_sequence([float(v) for v in a])
         if math.isfinite(es) and es < best[0]:
            best[0]=float(es); best[1]=a.copy()
    except Exception:
      pass
```
If best[0] initialized inf. Good.

Softmax:
```
def _softmax(x):
  x=np.asarray(x,float)
  y=x-np.max(x)
  e=np.exp(y)
  s=e.sum()
  if not math.isfinite(s) or s<=0:
     return np.ones(x.size)/x.size
  return e/s
```

Build A:
```
def _build_A(p):
  n=p.size
  A=np.zeros((2*n-1,n), dtype=np.float64)
  # local variable for speed
  for j in range(n):
      A[j:j+n, j] = p
  return A
```
Could use `for j, col in enumerate(A.T): A[j:j+n,col]=p` but A.T view? Better direct.

LP minmax:
```
def _lp_minmax(p):
   if linprog is None: return None
   n=p.size
   try:
     A=_build_A(p)
     rows=A.shape[0]
     neg=np.full((rows,1), -1.0)
     A_ub=np.hstack((A, neg))
     c=np.zeros(n+1); c[-1]=1.0
     eq_coeffs=np.ones(n+1); eq_coeffs[-1]=0.0
     bounds=[(0.0, None)]*(n+1)
     res=linprog(c, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=np.zeros(rows), A_eq=eq_coeffs.reshape(1,-1), b_eq=np.array([1.0]), bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'presolve': True, 'output_flag': False})
     if res is None or res.status != 0 or res.x is None: return None
     g=np.asarray(res.x[:n], dtype=np.float64)
     g=np.nan_to_num(g, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
     g=np.clip(g,0.0,None)
     sg=float(np.sum(g))
     if not math.isfinite(sg) or sg<=1e-15: return None
     return g/sg
   except Exception:
     return None
```
`output_flag` maybe invalid? HiGHS options allow? SciPy linprog options for method highs: 'presolve', 'dual', 'primal', 'scale', 'time_limit', 'output_flag'? I think yes? To be safe, use only `{'presolve': True}`. If invalid, it may raise? We can catch. Use options = {"presolve": True}. l也无害? Maybe `output_flag` accepted. I'll use `{'presolve': True}`.

def _lp_cap(p, M):
   if linprog is None or M<=0: return None
   try:
      A=_build_A(p); rows=A.shape[0]
      c=-np.ones(p.size)
      res=linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=np.full(rows,float(M)), bounds=[(0.0,None)]*p.size, method='highs', options={'presolve': True})
      if res is None or res.status !=0 or res.x is None: return None
      g=...
      sg=sum
      # to avoid unbounded returning huge? status 3 unbounded? If unbounded, maybe optimize? If status 3, res.x may None. return None.
      return g/sg
   except: return None
```
If M very small, optimum may be at a vertex with g unbounded? But as argued likely bounded. If unbounded, status !=0 return None.

Line search:
```
def _line_search(p, q, c_p=None, max_points=40):
  p=np.asarray(p,float); q=np.asarray(q,float)
  n=p.size
  sp=p.sum(); sq=q.sum()
  if sp<=0 or sq<=0: return None
  if sp!=1: p=p/sp
  if sq!=1: q=q/sq
  L=2*n-1
  if c_p is None or c_p.shape[0]!=L: c_p=np.convolve(p,p)
  c_q=np.convolve(q,q)
  c_m=np.convolve(p,q)
  def eval_ms(t):
     return ((1-t)**2)*c_p + (2*t*(1-t))*c_m + (t*t)*c_q
  taus=np.array([0.0,0.001,0.002,0.005,0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5,0.75,1.0])
  best_m=math.inf; best_tau=0.0
  # evaluate all
  for refine in range(2):
      vals=np.empty_like(taus)
      for i,t in enumerate(taus):
         vals[i]=float(np.max(eval_ms(t)))
      idx=int(np.argmin(vals))
      best_m=float(vals[idx]); best_tau=float(taus[idx])
      if best_m <= M? no
      if len(taus) < 3: break
      if idx == 0: lo,hi=0.0, taus[1]
      elif idx == len(taus)-1: lo,hi=taus[-2], 1.0
      else: lo,hi=taus[idx-1], taus[idx+1]
      if hi <= lo: break
      if refine==0:
         taus=np.linspace(lo,hi,33)
      else:
         # second refine narrower? Already around min; can break
         # Maybe set taus = linspace(lo,hi,21) and break after
         taus=np.linspace(lo,hi,21)
         # after this break
  # candidate
  t=best_tau
  cand=(1-t)*p + t*q
  s=cand.sum()
  if s<=0: cand=p
  else: cand=cand/s
  sc=_fast_score(cand)
  return cand, sc
```
We do two refine; after second break? The loop will compute second refined and then third if we don't break. Use:
```
for step in range(3):
   ...
   if step==2: break
   lo,hi...
   taus = np.linspace(lo,hi, 40 if step==0 else 25)
```
Good.
But note at step 0 if min at interior; lo/hi are neighboring base points. Then step1 refine 40 points. Step2 refine 25. Good.

Performance: For each step, loop over tau computing alloc; 13+40+25=78 vector ops each 191; fine.

Return candidate. If tau=0, cand=p, sc=local. In caller check improve.

LP refine:
```
def _lp_refine(p0, deadline, rng, best, tag=''):
  p=_normalize1(p0)
  local=_fast_score(p)
  _try_update(best,p)
  n=p.size
  target_r=TARGET/(2.0*n)
  stall=0
  it=0
  best_local=local; best_p=p.copy()
  while time.monotonic() < deadline - 1:
    it +=1
    if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 1: break
    c_p=np.convolve(p,p)
    M=float(np.max(c_p))
    if not math.isfinite(M) or M<=0: break
    local_current=2*n*M # may equal fast
    improved=False
    g=None
    mode=stall % 4
    if mode == 0:
       g=_lp_minmax(p)
    elif mode == 1:
       Mcap = M * 0.995
       # if target much lower, try target
       if target_r < Mcap and target_r > 0:
           Mcap = max(target_r*1.001, M*0.99)
       if Mcap <=0: Mcap=M*0.99
       g=_lp_cap(p, Mcap)
    elif mode == 2:
       Mcap = M * 0.985
       if target_r < M and target_r*1.002 < Mcap:
           Mcap = max(target_r*1.002, M*0.975)
       g=_lp_cap(p, Mcap)
    else:
       # very small cap near target
       Mcap = max(target_r*0.998 if target_r>0 else 1e-12, M*0.97)
       if Mcap >= M: Mcap = M*0.97
       g=_lp_cap(p, Mcap)
    if g is not None:
       q=_normalize1(g)
       # if q is too close skip? line search will no improve; but cost small
       if np.linalg.norm(q-p) > 1e-9:
           cand, sc = _line_search(p,q,c_p=c_p)
           if cand is not None and finite sc and sc < local - 1e-12:
               p=cand; local=sc; stall=0
               _try_update(best,p)
               best_local=local; best_p=p.copy()
               improved=True
    if not improved:
       stall += 1
       if stall % 5 ==0:
          # try perturb from best_p
          for noise in (0.03,0.08,0.2):
             pp=best_p*np.exp(noise*rng.standard_normal(n))
             pp=_normalize1(pp)
             ps=_fast_score(pp)
             if math.isfinite(ps) and ps < best_local*1.005:
                 p=pp; local=ps; stall=1; break
       if local > best_local + 1e-12:
          p=best_p.copy(); local=best_local
       if stall >= 30:
          break
    if it >= 10000: break
  _try_update(best,p)
  return p
```
But note in mode 1/2/3, `_lp_cap(p, Mcap)` with Mcap smaller than current M; feasible g but maybe sum 0? If no solution, stall. Fine.

However if mode 1/2/3 and Mcap < minimal? Cap LP with sum objective can always choose tiny g? Since no sum constraint, if M very small, g scaled down; objective maximize sum -> could be unbounded if there is direction with A g=0 and positive? For nonnegative A, if a column has all zero entries, unbounded. Could happen if some p_k zeros? Column j contains p_i for all i? Column j is p shifted down: entries from row j to j+n-1 equal p_0..p_{n-1}. If p has any positive, every column has positive entries? Column j entries are p_i for i=0..n-1 at rows j+i; all p_i present exactly once, so if p positive anywhere, column has positive; constraints bound each g_j by M/min p positive in rows? Row for which that entry used may be zero? If p_i positive, row j+i bounds g_j times p_i. So bounded. Good.

Potential issue: The cap LP maximizes sum g, but if M is current M*0.99, the feasible set includes 0; optimum finite positive. If M small, optimum g may be very small (scale M) and after normalization same shape. Fine.

Maybe cap LP with M below current will produce g with sum much less than 1; after normalization it's a direction. Good.

Could use the objective maximize sum with cap to produce a direction that is more mass in low A regions, which may be beneficial.

But maybe minmax LP is enough. The cap variants may consume time. We can reduce variants: mode 0 minmax, mode 1 cap 0.98M, mode 2 cap target, mode 3 minmax again. Good.

Now, L-BFGS polish:
```
def _lbfgs_polish(p0, deadline, rng, best):
   if minimize is None: return p0
   p=_normalize1(p0); local=_fast_score(p); _try_update
   n=p.size; L=2*n-1
   x=np.log(p+1e-300)
   best_local=local; best_p=p.copy()
   # current max for mu
   factors=[0.05,0.02,0.008,0.003,0.001,0.0004,0.00015]
   for f in factors:
      if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 10: break
      c=np.convolve(p,p); Mc=float(np.max(c))
      mu=f*Mc
      if not math.isfinite(mu) or mu<=0: mu=1e-8
      def fun(xval):
         pv=_softmax(xval)
         cval=np.convolve(pv,pv)
         shift=float(np.max(cval))
         z=(cval-shift)/mu
         # z<=0 but numerical; clip below -50
         z=np.clip(z, -50.0, 0.0)
         e=np.exp(z)
         es=float(np.sum(e))
         if es <=0 or not math.isfinite(es):
             w=np.zeros(L); w[int(np.argmax(cval))]=1.0; val=shift
         else:
             w=e/es
             val=mu*(math.log(es) + shift)
         gp=2.0*np.correlate(w, pv, mode='valid')
         D=float(np.dot(pv,gp))
         gx=pv*(gp - D)
         # clean gx
         gx=np.nan_to_num(gx, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
         return float(val), gx
      try:
         res=minimize(fun, x, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter':35, 'ftol':1e-12, 'gtol':1e-7, 'maxfun':120})
      except Exception:
         break
      if res is None or getattr(res,'x',None) is None:
         break
      q=_softmax(res.x)
      cand, sc = _line_search(p,q)
      if cand is not None and math.isfinite(sc) and sc < local - 1e-12:
         p=cand; local=sc; x=np.log(p+1e-300); _try_update(best,p)
         best_local=local; best_p=p.copy()
      else:
         # no improvement, but maybe res.x has lower smooth; still could be direction. If no exact improve, break? continue with smaller mu? might not help.
         # don't change p; break maybe because smaller mu only sharper.
         break
      if local <= TARGET and (TARGET - local) < 1e-5:
         # continue with smaller mu maybe
         pass
   _try_update(best,p)
   return p
```
One issue: In `fun`, `np.clip(z, -50, 0)` clips positive due roundoff; sum e >=1. Good. If `gv` length? `np.correlate(w,pv,'valid')` requires len w >= len pv; L=2n-1 >= n. OK.

The line search from p to q where q is L-BFGS result. If q worse, skip. Good.

But if `minimize` returns x that is far and q poor, line search may find small tau improvement. Good.

Now mirror refine:
```
def _mirror_refine(p0, deadline, rng, best):
  p=_normalize1(p0); local=_fast_score(p); _try_update
  n=p.size
  x=np.log(p+1e-300)
  c=np.convolve(p,p); Mc=max; mu=0.05*Mc
  stall=0; it=0
  best_local=local; best_p=p.copy()

def _interp(p,n): ...
def _make_starts(n, parent, best_arr, rng):
   starts=[]
   def add(a):
      a=_clean_arr(a)
      if a.size != n: 
         if a.size>1: a=np.interp(np.linspace(0,1,n), np.linspace(0,1,a.size), a)
         else: a=np.full(n, float(a[0]) if a.size else 1)
      a=_normalize1(a)
      # ensure not all equal? add anyway
      starts.append(a)
   add(np.ones(n))
   if parent is not None: add(parent)
   if best_arr is not None: add(best_arr)
   # power laws
   idx=np.arange(n, dtype=float)
   for shift in (0.5,1.0,1.5,2.0):
      for alpha in (0.35,0.4,0.45,0.5,0.55,0.6):
         add((idx+shift)**(-alpha))
   # differences of powers: (sqrt(idx+shift+1)-sqrt(idx+shift))
   for shift in (0.5,1.0,2.0):
      add(np.sqrt(idx+shift+1.0)-np.sqrt(idx+shift))
   # exponential/sinus
   x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   for c in (-2,-1,-0.5,0.5,1,2):
      add(np.exp(c*(x-0.5)))
   for k in (1,2,3,5):
      add(1+0.7*np.sin(2*math.pi*k*(x-0.5)))
      add(1+0.7*np.cos(2*math.pi*k*(x-0.5)-math.pi/4))
   # random dirs
   for alpha in (0.2,0.5,1,3):
      try: add(rng.dirichlet(np.full(n, alpha)))
      except: pass
   # maybe best shifted/reversed?
   # score sort
   scored=[]
   seen=[]? not needed
   for a in starts:
      sc=_fast_score(a)
      if math.isfinite(sc): scored.append((sc,a))
   scored.sort(key=lambda z:z[0])
   # reduce duplicates? Could keep first few if same? not important.
   return [a for _,a in scored[:12]]
```

Now `optimize_n`:
```
def _optimize_n(n, deadline, rng, best, parent):
    if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 15: return
    starts=_make_starts(n,parent,best[1],rng)
    if not starts: starts=[np.ones(n)/n]
    total = deadline - time.monotonic()
    fracs = [0.68,0.10,0.06,0.05,0.04,0.03,0.02,0.02]
    for rank, s in enumerate(starts):
       if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 15: break
       if rank >= len(fracs): time_left? use 0.02
       frac = fracs[rank] if rank < len(fracs) else 0.015
       # cap per start at remaining but ensure at least 15
       allowed = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + max(15.0, frac*total*0.9))
       # do not exceed deadline - 5
       _local_refine(s, allowed, rng, best)
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 20:
       # polish best interpolated to n
       p=_interp(best[1], n)
       _local_refine(p, deadline, rng, best)
```
Need if total fraction per rank uses total at start of optimize, not remaining. Good.

```
def _local_refine(p0, dl, rng, best):
    p=_normalize1(p0)
    local=_fast_score(p)
    _try_update_best(best,p)
    now=time.monotonic()
    if dl <= now+2: return p
    total=dl-now
    # LP 70%
    if total > 15:
       lp_dl=min(dl, now + 0.72*total)
       p=_lp_refine(p, lp_dl, rng,best)
    total=dl-time.monotonic()
    if total > 40 and p.size <= 128:
       lb_dl=min(dl, time.monotonic()+0.30*total)
       p=_lbfgs_polish(p, lb_dl, rng,best)
    total=dl-time.monotonic()
    if total > 20 and p.size <= 160:
       mir_dl=min(dl, time.monotonic()+0.40*total)
       p=_mirror_refine(p, mir_dl, rng,best)
    total=dl-time.monotonic()
    if total > 20:
       p=_lp_refine(p, min(dl, time.monotonic()+total*0.6), rng,best)
    _try_update_best(best,p)
    return p
```

import numpy as np
import time
import math

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog, minimize
except Exception:
    linprog = None
    minimize = None

TARGET = 1.50274349

def _clean_arr(x):
    try:
       a = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
       a = np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
       a = np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return a

def _normalize1(a):
    a = _clean_arr(a)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-15:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return a / s

def _fast_score(a): ...
```

```
def _interp_seq(arr, n):
   arr=_clean_arr(arr)
   if arr.size == n: return _normalize1(arr)
   if arr.size <=0: return np.ones(n)/n
   old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,arr.size)
   new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
   g=np.interp(new, old, arr)
   return _normalize1(g)
```

```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog, minimize
except Exception:
    linprog = None
    minimize = None

TARGET = 1.50274349


def _clean_arr(x):
    try:
        a = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        a = np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        a = np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return a


def _normalize1(a):
    a = _clean_arr(a)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-15:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return a / s


def _fast_score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return math.inf
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-15:
        return math.inf
    a = a / s
    c = np.convolve(a, a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return math.inf
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not math.isfinite(m):
        return math.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * m)


def _try_update_best(best, cand):
    try:
        a = _clean_arr(cand)
        if a.size == 0:
            return
        s = float(np.sum(a))
        if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-15:
            return
        a = a / s
        fs = _fast_score(a)
        if math.isfinite(fs) and fs < best[0] - 1e-12:
            es = evaluate_sequence([float(v) for v in a])
            if math.isfinite(es) and es < best[0]:
                best[0] = float(es)
                best[1] = a.copy()
    except Exception:
        pass


def _softmax(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    y = x - float(np.max(x))
    e = np.exp(y)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(x.size, dtype=np.float64) / x.size
    return e / s


def _interp_seq(arr, n):
    arr = _clean_arr(arr)
    if arr.size == 0:
        return np.ones(int(n), dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 1:
        g = np.array([float(arr[0])] if arr.size else [1.0], dtype=np.float64)
        return g
    if arr.size == 1:
        g = np.full(n, float(arr[0]), dtype=np.float64)
        return g
    if arr.size == n:
        return _normalize1(arr)
    old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, arr.size)
    new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    g = np.interp(new, old, arr)
    return _normalize1(g)


def _build_A(p):
    n = int(p.size)
    A = np.zeros((2 * n - 1, n), dtype=np.float64)
    for j in range(n):
        A[j:j + n, j] = p
    return A


def _lp_minmax(p):
    if linprog is None:
        return None
    try:
        n = int(p.size)
        A = _build_A(p)
        rows = A.shape[0]

        A_ub = np.empty((rows, n + 1), dtype=np.float64)
        A_ub[:, :n] = A
        A_ub[:, n] = -1.0

        c = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
        c[-1] = 1.0

        A_eq = np.ones(n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
        A_eq[-1] = 0.0

        bounds = [(0.0, None)] * (n + 1)

        res = linprog(
            c,
            A_ub=A_ub,
            b_ub=np.zeros(rows, dtype=np.float64),
            A_eq=A_eq.reshape(1, -1),
            b_eq=np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64),
            bounds=bounds,
            method="highs",
            options={"presolve": True},
        )
        if res is None or getattr(res, "status", None) != 0 or getattr(res, "x", None) is None:
            return None
        g = np.asarray(res.x[:n], dtype=np.float64)
        g = np.nan_to_num(g, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        g = np.clip(g, 0.0, None)
        sg = float(np.sum(g))
        if not math.isfinite(sg) or sg <= 1e-15:
            return None
        return g / sg
    except Exception:
        return None


def _lp_cap(p, M):
    if linprog is None:
        return None
    try:
        M = float(M)
        if not math.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
            return None
        n = int(p.size)
        A = _build_A(p)
        rows = A.shape[0]

        res = linprog(
            -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64),
            A_ub=A,
            b_ub=np.full(rows, M, dtype=np.float64),
            bounds=[(0.0, None)] * n,
            method="highs",
            options={"presolve": True},
        )
        if res is None or getattr(res, "status", None) != 0 or getattr(res, "x", None) is None:
            return None
        g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
        g = np.nan_to_num(g, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        g = np.clip(g, 0.0, None)
        sg = float(np.sum(g))
        if not math.isfinite(sg) or sg <= 1e-15:
            return None
        return g / sg
    except Exception:
        return None


def _line_search(p, q, c_p=None):
    p = _normalize1(p)
    q = _normalize1(q)
    n = p.size
    L = 2 * n - 1

    if c_p is None or c_p.size != L:
        c_p = np.convolve(p, p)
    c_q = np.convolve(q, q)
    c_m = np.convolve(p, q)

    def eval_max(t):
        t = float(t)
        a = (1.0 - t) * (1.0 - t)
        b = 2.0 * t * (1.0 - t)
        d = t * t
        return float(np.max(a * c_p + b * c_m + d * c_q))

    taus = np.array(
        [0.0, 0.0005, 0.001, 0.002, 0.005, 0.01, 0.02, 0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.3, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0],
        dtype=np.float64,
    )

    best_tau = 0.0
    best_m = math.inf

    for stage in range(3):
        vals = np.empty(taus.size, dtype=np.float64)
        for i, t in enumerate(taus):
            vals[i] = eval_max(t)
        idx = int(np.argmin(vals))
        best_m = float(vals[idx])
        best_tau = float(taus[idx])

        if stage == 2:
            break

        if idx == 0:
            lo, hi = float(taus[0]), float(taus[1])
        elif idx == taus.size - 1:
            lo, hi = float(taus[-2]), float(taus[-1])
        else:
            lo, hi = float(taus[idx - 1]), float(taus[idx + 1])

        if hi <= lo:
            break
        if stage == 0:
            taus = np.linspace(lo, hi, 37, dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            taus = np.linspace(lo, hi, 25, dtype=np.float64)

    cand = (1.0 - best_tau) * p + best_tau * q
    s = float(np.sum(cand))
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-15:
        cand = p
    else:
        cand = cand / s

    return cand, float(2.0 * n * best_m)


def _safe_perturb(best_p, rng, sigma_sequences):
    n = int(best_p.size)
    for sigma in sigma_sequences:
        try:
            z = sigma * rng.standard_normal(n)
            z = np.clip(z, -10.0, 10.0)
            pp = best_p * np.exp(z)
            pp = _normalize1(pp)
            yield pp
        except Exception:
            continue


def _lp_refine(p0, deadline, rng, best):
    p = _normalize1(p0)
    n = int(p.size)
    local = _fast_score(p)
    _try_update_best(best, p)

    target_r = TARGET / (2.0 * float(n))
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_local = local
    stall = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 1.0:
        it += 1
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 1.0:
            break
        if it > 12000:
            break

        c_p = np.convolve(p, p)
        M = float(np.max(c_p))
        if not math.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
            break

        improved = False
        mode = stall % 4

        g = None
        if mode == 0:
            g = _lp_minmax(p)
        elif mode == 1:
            g = _lp_cap(p, M)
        elif mode == 2:
            Mc = M * 0.99
            if M > target_r * 1.05:
                Mc = max(target_r * 1.001, Mc)
            if Mc >= M:
                Mc = M * 0.99
            g = _lp_cap(p, Mc)
        else:
            if M > target_r * 1.02:
                Mc = max(target_r * 1.001, M * 0.985)
            else:
                Mc = M * 0.985
            if Mc >= M:
                Mc = M * 0.985
            g = _lp_cap(p, Mc)

        if g is not None:
            q = _normalize1(g)
            if float(np.linalg.norm(q - p)) > 1e-9:
                cand, est = _line_search(p, q, c_p=c_p)
                if cand is not None and est < local - 1e-11:
                    sc = _fast_score(cand)
                    if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < local - 1e-12:
                        p = cand
                        local = sc
                        stall = 0
                        best_p = p.copy()
                        best_local = local
                        _try_update_best(best, p)
                        improved = True

        if not improved:
            stall += 1
            if stall % 5 == 0:
                for pp in _safe_perturb(best_p, rng, (0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.20)):
                    ps = _fast_score(pp)
                    if math.isfinite(ps) and ps < local - 1e-11:
                        p = pp
                        local = ps
                        stall = 1
                        best_p = p.copy()
                        best_local = local
                        _try_update_best(best, p)
                        break
            if stall >= 32:
                break

    _try_update_best(best, p)
    return p


def _lbfgs_polish(p0, deadline, rng, best):
    if minimize is None:
        return p0
    p = _normalize1(p0)
    n = int(p.size)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    local = _fast_score(p)
    _try_update_best(best, p)

    x = np.log(p + 1e-300)
    fails = 0

    for f in (0.05, 0.02, 0.008, 0.003, 0.001, 0.0004, 0.00015):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 12.0:
            break

        c0 = np.convolve(p, p)
        M0 = float(np.max(c0))
        mu = f * M0
        if not math.isfinite(mu) or mu <= 0.0:
            mu = 1e-8

        def fun(xval, mu=mu):
            pv = _softmax(xval)
            cv = np.convolve(pv, pv)
            sh = float(np.max(cv))
            zz = (cv - sh) / mu
            zz = np.clip(zz, -50.0, 0.0)
            ee = np.exp(zz)
            ss = float(np.sum(ee))
            if not math.isfinite(ss) or ss <= 0.0:
                w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
                w[int(np.argmax(cv))] = 1.0
                val = sh
            else:
                w = ee / ss
                val = mu * (math.log(ss) + sh)

            gp = 2.0 * np.correlate(w, pv, mode="valid")
            D = float(np.dot(pv, gp))
            gx = pv * (gp - D)
            gx = np.nan_to_num(gx, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
            return float(val), gx

        try:
            res = minimize(
                fun,
                x,
                jac=True,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                options={"maxiter": 35, "ftol": 1e-12, "gtol": 1e-7, "maxfun": 120},
            )
        except Exception:
            break

        if res is None or getattr(res, "x", None) is None:
            break

        q = _softmax(res.x)
        cand, est = _line_search(p, q)
        if cand is not None and est < local - 1e-11:
            sc = _fast_score(cand)
            if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < local - 1e-12:
                p = cand
                local = sc
                x = np.log(p + 1e-300)
                _try_update_best(best, p)
                fails = 0
                continue
        fails += 1
        if fails >= 2:
            break

    _try_update_best(best, p)
    return p


def _mirror_refine(p0, deadline, rng, best):
    p = _normalize1(p0)
    n = int(p.size)
    local = _fast_score(p)
    _try_update_best(best, p)

    x = np.log(p + 1e-300)
    c = np.convolve(p, p)
    M = float(np.max(c))
    mu = 0.05 * M if M > 0.0 else 1e-8

    stall = 0
    it = 0
    etas = (1e-4, 3e-4, 1e-3, 3e-3, 1e-2, 3e-2, 0.1, 0.3, 1.0)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 1.0:
        it += 1
        if it > 6000:
            break

        c = np.convolve(p, p)
        M = float(np.max(c))
        if not math.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
            break

        mu = max(1e-10, min(mu, 0.05 * M))
        if stall > 0 and stall % 7 == 0:
            mu = max(1e-10, mu * 0.85)

        z = (c - M) / mu
        z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 0.0)
        e = np.exp(z)
        s = float(np.sum(e))
        if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
            w = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
            w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        else:
            w = e / s

        gp = 2.0 * np.correlate(w, p, mode="valid")
        gmax = float(np.max(gp))
        if not math.isfinite(gmax) or gmax <= 1e-300:
            break
        d = gp / gmax

        best_cand = p
        best_sc = local
        for eta in etas:
            try:
                y = x - eta * d
                ym = float(np.max(y))
                if not math.isfinite(ym):
                    continue
                yy = y - ym
                ee = np.exp(yy)
                ss = float(np.sum(ee))
                if not math.isfinite(ss) or ss <= 0.0:
                    continue
                q = ee / ss
                sc = _fast_score(q)
                if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-13:
                    best_cand = q
                    best_sc = sc
            except Exception:
                continue

        if best_cand is not p:
            p = best_cand
            local = best_sc
            x = np.log(p + 1e-300)
            stall = 0
            mu = max(1e-10, mu * 0.9)
            _try_update_best(best, p)
            continue

        stall += 1
        if stall % 5 == 0:
            for pp in _safe_perturb(p, rng, (0.02, 0.05, 0.10)):
                ps = _fast_score(pp)
                if math.isfinite(ps) and ps < local - 1e-11:
                    p = pp
                    local = ps
                    x = np.log(p + 1e-300)
                    stall = 1
                    _try_update_best(best, p)
                    break

        if stall > 45:
            break

    _try_update_best(best, p)
    return p


def _local_refine(p0, dl, rng, best):
    p = _normalize1(p0)
    _try_update_best(best, p)

    now = time.monotonic()
    if dl <= now + 2.0:
        return p
    total = dl - now

    if total > 12.0:
        p = _lp_refine(p, min(dl, now + 0.72 * total), rng, best)

    total = dl - time.monotonic()
    if total > 35.0 and p.size <= 128:
        p = _lbfgs_polish(p, min(dl, time.monotonic() + 0.30 * total), rng, best)

    total = dl - time.monotonic()
    if total > 18.0 and p.size <= 160:
        p = _mirror_refine(p, min(dl, time.monotonic() + 0.45 * total), rng, best)

    total = dl - time.monotonic()
    if total > 25.0:
        p = _lp_refine(p, min(dl, time.monotonic() + min(total, dl - time.monotonic())), rng, best)

    _try_update_best(best, p)
    return p


def _make_starts(n, parent, best_arr, rng):
    n = int(n)
    starts = []

    def add(a):
        try:
            if a is None:
                return
            g = _interp_seq(a, n)
            g = _normalize1(g)
            if g.size == n:
                starts.append(g)
        except Exception:
            pass

    add(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
    if parent is not None:
        add(parent)
    if best_arr is not None:
        add(best_arr)
        add(_interp_seq(np.asarray(best_arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()[::-1], n))

    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)

    for shift in (0.5, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0):
        for alpha in (0.35, 0.40, 0.45, 0.50, 0.55, 0.60):
            add((idx + shift) ** (-alpha))

    for shift in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
        add(np.sqrt(idx + shift + 1.0) - np.sqrt(idx + shift))

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    for cc in (-2.0, -1.0, -0.5, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
        add(np.exp(cc * (x - 0.5)))
    for k in (1, 2, 3, 5):
        add(1.0 + 0.7 * np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * k * (x - 0.5)))
        add(1.0 + 0.7 * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * k * (x - 0.5) - 0.4))

    for alpha in (0.2, 0.5, 1.0, 3.0):
        try:
            add(rng.dirichlet(np.full(n, float(alpha))))
        except Exception:
            pass

    scored = []
    seen = set()
    for a in starts:
        key = tuple(np.round(a, 10))
        if key in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(key)
        sc = _fast_score(a)
        if math.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append((sc, a))

    if not scored:
        u = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
        scored.append((_fast_score(u), u))

    scored.sort(key=lambda z: z[0])
    return [a for _, a in scored[:12]]


def _optimize_n(n, deadline, rng, best, parent):
    n = int(n)
    if n < 2 or time.monotonic() >= deadline - 10.0:
        return
    if n > 320:
        n = 320

    starts = _make_starts(n, parent, best[1], rng)
    if not starts:
        starts = [np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n]

    initial_total = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    fracs = (0.70, 0.10, 0.06, 0.04, 0.03, 0.03, 0.02, 0.02)

    for rank, st in enumerate(starts):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 12.0:
            break
        frac = fracs[rank] if rank < len(fracs) else 0.015
        per = max(10.0, frac * initial_total)
        allowed = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + per)
        if allowed <= time.monotonic() + 6.0:
            continue
        _local_refine(st, allowed, rng, best)

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 20.0:
        p = _interp_seq(best[1], n)
        _local_refine(p, deadline, rng, best)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t0 = time.monotonic()

    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 1300.0
    if not math.isfinite(budget) or budget <= 0.0:
        budget = 1300.0

    headroom = min(45.0, max(2.0, 0.05 * budget))
    deadline = t0 + max(1.0, budget - headroom)

    best = [math.inf, np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)]
    parent = None

    try:
        h = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if h is not None:
            arr = _clean_arr(h)
            if arr.size > 0:
                s = float(np.sum(arr))
                if math.isfinite(s) and s > 1e-15:
                    arr = arr / s
                parent = arr
                _try_update_best(best, parent)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if not math.isfinite(best[0]):
        n0 = 96
        fb = _normalize1((np.arange(n0, dtype=np.float64) + 1.0) ** (-0.5))
        _try_update_best(best, fb)
        if parent is None:
            parent = fb

    if not math.isfinite(best[0]):
        best[0] = 2.0
        best[1] = np.full(96, 1.0 / 96.0, dtype=np.float64)

    now = time.monotonic()
    rem = deadline - now
    if rem > 20.0:
        if parent is not None and parent.size >= 2 and parent.size <= 320:
            n1 = int(parent.size)
        else:
            n1 = 96

        alloc1 = min(rem * 0.60, 800.0)
        alloc1 = max(30.0, alloc1)
        d1 = min(deadline, now + alloc1)
        _optimize_n(n1, d1, rng, best, parent)

        candidates = []
        if n1 != 96:
            candidates.append(96)
        for m in (128, 192, 256):
            if m != n1:
                candidates.append(m)

        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 90.0 and candidates:
            sel = candidates[:3]
            weights = [0.45, 0.30, 0.25]
            wsum = float(sum(weights[:len(sel)]))
            if wsum > 0.0:
                for i, m in enumerate(sel):
                    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
                    if rem < 60.0:
                        break
                    alloc = (rem - 20.0) * float(weights[i]) / wsum
                    if alloc < 25.0:
                        continue
                    start = time.monotonic()
                    d2 = min(deadline, start + alloc)
                    _optimize_n(m, d2, rng, best, parent)

    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 18.0:
        _local_refine(_normalize1(best[1]), deadline, rng, best)

    final = _normalize1(best[1])
    _try_update_best(best, final)
    final = np.asarray(best[1], dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    final = _clean_arr(final)
    if final.size == 0:
        final = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)

    try:
        fin_sc = evaluate_sequence([float(v) for v in final])
    except Exception:
        fin_sc = math.inf

    if not math.isfinite(fin_sc):
        fallback = _normalize1((np.arange(96, dtype=np.float64) + 1.0) ** (-0.5))
        final = fallback

    return [float(v) for v in final]
```
